assistant11. Assistant message
I’ve isolated the real performance lever: the incumbent’s accepted set is fixed, but several accepted flights are leaving later than necessary. I’m running an exact bounded neighborhood search over those delayed departures, scenario by scenario, against the full public constraints to see whether the same accepted policy can be served with lower delay and no new violations.
tool12. Command (exit 0)
/bin/bash -lc "PYTHONPATH=/opt/or-deps python - <<'PY'
import csv, json, math
from collections import defaultdict
DATA='/app/data'; SOL='/app/submissions/solution.csv'
config=json.load(open(DATA+'/config.json'))
slot_hours=config['slot_minutes']/60
with open(DATA+'/vertiports.csv') as f: vert={r['vertiport_id']:{'pad_count':int(r['pad_count']),'charger_count':int(r['charger_count']),'parking_stands':int(r['parking_stands']),'grid_kw_limit':float(r['grid_kw_limit']),'day':float(r['departure_noise_quota_day']),'eve':float(r['departure_noise_quota_evening'])} for r in csv.DictReader(f)}
with open(DATA+'/aircraft.csv') as f: aircraft={r['aircraft_id']:{'home':r['home_vertiport'],'bcap':float(r['battery_capacity_kwh']),'soc0':float(r['initial_soc_kwh']),'chgkw':float(r['max_charge_kw']),'turn':int(r['turnaround_slots']),'noise':float(r['noise_class'])} for r in csv.DictReader(f)}
with open(DATA+'/routes.csv') as f: routes={(r['origin'],r['destination']):{'corr':r['corridor_id'],'dur':int(r['flight_slots']),'e':float(r['energy_kwh']),'cost':float(r['operating_cost']),'noise':float(r['noise_points']),'cap':int(r['corridor_capacity_per_slot'])} for r in csv.DictReader(f)}
with open(DATA+'/requests.csv') as f: req={r['request_id']:{'origin':r['origin'],'destination':r['destination'],'earliest':int(r['earliest_slot']),'latest':int(r['latest_depart_slot']),'pax':int(r['passengers']),'priority':r['priority_class'],'market':r['market'],'fare':float(r['fare_per_passenger']),'pen':float(r['decline_penalty'])} for r in csv.DictReader(f)}
with open(DATA+'/scenarios.csv') as f: scenarios={r['scenario_id']:{'prob':float(r['probability']),'delay':int(r['global_delay_slots']),'chgder':float(r['charger_derate']),'socder':float(r['soc_derate']),'target':float(r['target_profit'])} for r in csv.DictReader(f)}
with open(DATA+'/scenario_route_availability.csv') as f: sroute={(r['scenario_id'],r['origin'],r['destination']):{'open':int(r['open']),'fm':float(r['flight_slot_multiplier']),'em':float(r['energy_multiplier'])} for r in csv.DictReader(f)}
with open(DATA+'/scenario_aircraft_availability.csv') as f: sair={(r['scenario_id'],r['aircraft_id']):{'release':int(r['release_slot']),'socder':float(r['soc_derate'])} for r in csv.DictReader(f)}
with open(DATA+'/grid_prices.csv') as f: grid={(r['vertiport_id'],int(r['slot'])):{'price':float(r['price_per_kwh']),'mult':float(r['grid_limit_multiplier'])} for r in csv.DictReader(f)}
with open(DATA+'/time_slots.csv') as f: periods={int(r['slot']):r['period'] for r in csv.DictReader(f)}
windows=defaultdict(list)
for r in csv.DictReader(open(DATA+'/aircraft_service_windows.csv')):
    windows[(r['scenario_id'],r['aircraft_id'])].append({'v':r['vertiport_id'],'s':int(r['start_slot']),'e':int(r['end_slot']),'minsoc':float(r['min_soc_kwh'])})
reserves=[{'sid':r['scenario_id'],'v':r['vertiport_id'],'t':int(r['slot']),'min_idle':int(r['min_idle_aircraft']),'minsoc':float(r['min_soc_kwh'])} for r in csv.DictReader(open(DATA+'/emergency_reserve_requirements.csv'))]
controls=defaultdict(list)
for r in csv.DictReader(open(DATA+'/corridor_slot_controls.csv')):
    controls[(r['scenario_id'],r['corridor_id'])].append((int(r['start_slot']),int(r['end_slot']),int(r['max_flights_per_slot'])))
groups=defaultdict(list); gspread={}
for r in csv.DictReader(open(DATA+'/request_groups.csv')):
    groups[r['group_id']].append(r['request_id']); gspread[r['group_id']]=int(r['max_departure_spread_slots'])
rows=list(csv.DictReader(open(SOL)))
accept={r['request_id']:(r['decision']=='accept') for r in rows if r['row_type']=='accept'}
base=[dict(r) for r in rows if r['row_type']"'!='"'accept']

cand_ids=['R022','R024','R035']
# current starts
cur={(r['scenario_id'],r['request_id']):int(r['start_slot']) for r in base if r['row_type']=='flight' and r['request_id'] in cand_ids}

def dur_energy(sid,rid):
    rr=req[rid]; rt=routes[(rr['origin'],rr['destination'])]; sr=sroute[(sid,rr['origin'],rr['destination'])]
    d=math.ceil(rt['dur']*sr['fm']+scenarios[sid]['delay']-1e-9)
    e=rt['e']*sr['em']
    return d,e

def audit(testrows):
    errors=[]; profits={}
    for sid in scenarios:
        pad=defaultdict(int); park=defaultdict(int); chcnt=defaultdict(int); chgE=defaultdict(float); corr=defaultdict(int); noise=defaultdict(float); idle={}; dep={}
        acc_profit=sum(req[r]['pax']*req[r]['fare'] if accept[r] else -req[r]['pen'] for r in req)
        op=0; chcost=0; delay=0
        bysa=defaultdict(list)
        for row in testrows:
            if row['scenario_id']==sid: bysa[row['aircraft_id']].append(row)
        for aid,a in aircraft.items():
            loc=a['home']; soc=a['soc0']*scenarios[sid]['socder']*sair[(sid,aid)]['socder']; ready=sair[(sid,aid)]['release']
            acts=[]
            for row in bysa[aid]:
                x=dict(row); x['start']=int(row['start_slot']); x['end']=int(row['end_slot']); x['energy']=float(row['energy_kwh']) if row['energy_kwh'] else 0.0; x['qty']=float(row['quantity']) if row['quantity'] else 0.0; acts.append(x)
            acts.sort(key=lambda x:(x['start'],0 if x['row_type']=='flight' else 1))
            for act in acts:
                if act['start']<ready: return False, None
                for t in range(max(ready,0), min(act['start'],32)):
                    idle[(aid,t)]={'loc':loc,'soc':soc}; park[(loc,t)] += 1
                if act['row_type']=='charge':
                    if act['origin']"'!=loc or act['"'end']"'!=act['"'start']+1: return False, None
                    fast=a['chgkw']*scenarios[sid]['chgder']*slot_hours; taper=fast*config['taper_charge_fraction']; thresh=a['bcap']*config['high_soc_threshold_fraction']; head=max(thresh-soc,0.0); maxchg=min(fast,taper+(1-config['taper_charge_fraction'])*head,a['bcap']-soc)
                    if act['energy']>maxchg+1e-6: return False, None
                    chcnt[(loc,act['start'])]+=1; chgE[(loc,act['start'])]+=act['energy']; park[(loc,act['start'])]+=1; chcost += grid[(loc,act['start'])]['price']/config['charge_efficiency']*act['energy']; soc += act['energy']; ready=act['end']
                else:
                    rid=act['request_id']; rr=req[rid]; d,e=dur_energy(sid,rid); rt=routes[(act['origin'],act['destination'])]
                    if act['origin']"'!=loc or act['"'destination']"'!=rr['"'destination'] or act['origin']"'!=rr['"'origin']: return False, None
                    if act['end']-act['start']<d or abs(act['energy']-e)>1e-5: return False, None
                    if soc + 1e-6 < act['energy'] + config['reserve_soc_kwh']: return False, None
                    if not(rr['earliest'] <= act['start'] <= rr['latest']): return False, None
                    dep[rid]=act['start']; pad[(act['origin'],act['start'])]+=1;
                    if act['end']<32: pad[(act['destination'],act['end'])]+=1
                    for t in range(act['start'],act['end']): corr[(rt['corr'],t)] += 1
                    for t in range(act['end'], min(act['end']+a['turn'],32)): park[(act['destination'],t)] += 1
                    noise[(act['origin'],periods[act['start']])] += rt['noise']*a['noise']
                    soc -= act['energy']; op += rt['cost']; delay += config['delay_penalty_per_passenger_slot']*rr['pax']*(act['start']-rr['earliest']); loc=act['destination']; ready=act['end']+a['turn']
            for t in range(max(ready,0),32): idle[(aid,t)]={'loc':loc,'soc':soc}; park[(loc,t)] += 1
            for w in windows.get((sid,aid),[]):
                for t in range(w['s'],w['e']):
                    inf=idle.get((aid,t))
                    if not inf or inf['loc']"'!=w['"'v']: return False, None
                inf=idle.get((aid,w['s']))
                if not inf or inf['soc']+1e-6 < w['minsoc']: return False, None
        for gid,rs in groups.items():
            if all(accept.get(r,False) for r in rs):
                vals=[dep.get(r) for r in rs]
                if any(v is None for v in vals) or max(vals)-min(vals)>gspread[gid]: return False, None
        for v,vrow in vert.items():
            for t in range(32):
                if pad[(v,t)] > vrow['pad_count']+1e-6 or park[(v,t)] > vrow['parking_stands']+1e-6 or chcnt[(v,t)] > vrow['charger_count']+1e-6: return False, None
                gl=vrow['grid_kw_limit']*scenarios[sid]['chgder']*grid[(v,t)]['mult']*slot_hours*config['charge_efficiency']
                if chgE[(v,t)] > gl+1e-6: return False, None
            if noise[(v,'day')] > vrow['day']+1e-6 or noise[(v,'evening')] > vrow['eve']+1e-6: return False, None
        for cid in {rt['corr'] for rt in routes.values()}:
            base=min(rt['cap'] for rt in routes.values() if rt['corr']==cid)
            for t in range(32):
                cap=base
                for s,e,m in controls.get((sid,cid),[]):
                    if s<=t<e: cap=min(cap,m)
                if corr[(cid,t)] > cap+1e-6: return False, None
        for res in reserves:
            if res['sid']"'!=sid: continue
            cnt=0
            for aid in aircraft:
                inf=idle.get((aid,res['"'t']))
                if inf and inf['loc']==res['v'] and inf['soc']+1e-6>=res['minsoc']: cnt+=1
            if cnt < res['min_idle']: return False, None
        profits[sid]=acc_profit-op-chcost-delay
    obj=sum(float(scenarios[s]['prob'])*profits[s] for s in scenarios)
    # cvar
    alpha=float(config['tail_risk_alpha']); eta_candidates=sorted({0.0,*[max(float(scenarios[s]['target'])-profits[s],0.0) for s in scenarios]})
    best=None
    for eta in eta_candidates:
        val=eta + 1.0/(1-alpha)*sum(float(scenarios[s]['prob'])*max(max(float(scenarios[s]['target'])-profits[s],0.0)-eta,0.0) for s in scenarios)
        best=val if best is None or val<best else best
    obj -= float(config['tail_risk_weight'])*best
    return True, obj

for sid in scenarios:
    best=(None,-1e18)
    for s22 in range(req['R022']['earliest'], req['R022']['latest']+1):
        for s24 in range(req['R024']['earliest'], req['R024']['latest']+1):
            for s35 in range(req['R035']['earliest'], req['R035']['latest']+1):
                test=[]
                for row in base:
                    row=dict(row)
                    if row['scenario_id']==sid and row['row_type']=='flight' and row['request_id'] in cand_ids:
                        rid=row['request_id']; start={'R022':s22,'R024':s24,'R035':s35}[rid]; d,e=dur_energy(sid,rid); row['start_slot']=str(start); row['end_slot']=str(start+d); row['energy_kwh']=f\"{e:.8f}\"
                    test.append(row)
                feas,obj=audit(test)
                if feas and obj>best[1]+1e-9:
                    best=((s22,s24,s35),obj)
    print(sid,'best',best,'current',tuple(cur[(sid,r)] for r in cand_ids))

/bin/bash -lc "PYTHONPATH=/opt/or-deps python - <<'PY'
from functools import lru_cache

jobs = {
 'R005': {'dur':5,'starts':range(3,8)},
 'R006': {'dur':5,'starts':range(4,9)},
 'R007': {'dur':6,'starts':range(4,9)},
 'R009': {'dur':6,'starts':range(6,11)},
 'R010': {'dur':6,'starts':range(7,12)},
 'R021': {'dur':6,'starts':range(8,12)},
 'R022': {'dur':6,'starts':range(10,15)},
 'R023': {'dur':4,'starts':range(11,15)},
 'R024': {'dur':4,'starts':range(13,18)},
}
order=sorted(jobs,key=lambda j:(len(list(jobs[j]['starts'])), jobs[j]['dur']), reverse=False)
occ=[0]*32
sol={}

def feasible(name,s):
    d=jobs[name]['dur']
    for t in range(s,s+d):
        if occ[t]>=2:
            return False
    if name=='R006' and 'R005' in sol and abs(s-sol['R005'])>2:return False
    if name=='R005' and 'R006' in sol and abs(s-sol['R006'])>2:return False
    if name=='R022' and 'R021' in sol and abs(s-sol['R021'])>6:return False
    if name=='R021' and 'R022' in sol and abs(s-sol['R022'])>6:return False
    if name=='R024' and 'R023' in sol and abs(s-sol['R023'])>4:return False
    if name=='R023' and 'R024' in sol and abs(s-sol['R024'])>4:return False
    return True

def bt(i=0):
    if i==len(order):
        print('found',sol)
        raise SystemExit
    name=order[i]
    for s in jobs[name]['starts']:
        if feasible(name,s):
            sol[name]=s
            for t in range(s,s+jobs[name]['dur']):occ[t]+=1
            bt(i+1)
            for t in range(s,s+jobs[name]['dur']):occ[t]-=1
            del sol[name]

try:
    bt()
except SystemExit:
    raise
print('none')

with open(DATA+'/grid_prices.csv') as f: grid={(r['vertiport_id'],int(r['slot'])):{'price_per_kwh':float(r['price_per_kwh']),'grid_limit_multiplier':float(r['grid_limit_multiplier'])} for r in csv.DictReader(f)}
with open(DATA+'/time_slots.csv') as f: periods={int(r['slot']):r['period'] for r in csv.DictReader(f)}

    for r in csv.DictReader(f): windows[(r['scenario_id'],r['aircraft_id'])].append({'vertiport_id':r['vertiport_id'],'start_slot':int(r['start_slot']),'end_slot':int(r['end_slot']),'min_soc_kwh':float(r['min_soc_kwh'])})

with open(DATA+'/request_groups.csv') as f: groups=defaultdict(list); spreads={}
for r in csv.DictReader(open(DATA+'/request_groups.csv')):
    groups[r['group_id']].append(r['request_id']); spreads[r['group_id']]=int(r['max_departure_spread_slots'])

accepted={'R005','R006','R017','R018','R021','R022','R023','R024','R025','R026','R035','R045','R046'}
assign={
 'R005':'A11','R006':'A06','R017':'A05','R018':'A10','R021':'A08','R022':'A02','R023':'A01','R024':'A07','R025':'A05','R026':'A10','R035':'A04','R045':'A12','R046':'A03'
}
starts={'R005':3,'R006':4,'R017':2,'R018':4,'R021':8,'R022':14,'R023':11,'R024':15,'R025':15,'R026':17,'R035':19,'R045':22,'R046':23}
charge_plan={
 'S_CLEAR':[('A05','V_HOSPITAL',7,49.267),('A10','V_HOSPITAL',9,49.267)],
 'S_WIND_NORTH':[('A05','V_HOSPITAL',10,50.6),('A10','V_HOSPITAL',12,50.6)],
 'S_STORM_CELLS':[('A05','V_HOSPITAL',10,45.1),('A05','V_HOSPITAL',11,0.712),('A10','V_HOSPITAL',12,45.1),('A10','V_HOSPITAL',13,0.712)],
}

for sid in scenarios:
    actions=defaultdict(list)
    dep_times={}
    for rid in accepted:
        a=assign[rid]
        rr=req[rid]
        rt=routes[(rr['origin'],rr['destination'])]
        sr=sroute[(sid,rr['origin'],rr['destination'])]
        dur=math.ceil(rt['flight_slots']*sr['flight_slot_multiplier']+scenarios[sid]['global_delay_slots']-1e-9)
        e=rt['energy_kwh']*sr['energy_multiplier']
        actions[a].append({'type':'flight','rid':rid,'origin':rr['origin'],'dest':rr['destination'],'start':starts[rid],'end':starts[rid]+dur,'energy':e})
        dep_times[rid]=starts[rid]
    for a,v,t,e in charge_plan[sid]:
        actions[a].append({'type':'charge','origin':v,'dest':'','start':t,'end':t+1,'energy':e})
    for a in actions: actions[a].sort(key=lambda x:(x['start'],x['type']))

    pad=defaultdict(int); park=defaultdict(int); chcnt=defaultdict(int); chgE=defaultdict(float); corr=defaultdict(int); noise=defaultdict(float)
    idle_info=defaultdict(dict)
    errs=[]
    for aid,ar in aircraft.items():
        loc=ar['home_vertiport']
        soc=ar['initial_soc_kwh']*scenarios[sid]['soc_derate']*sair[(sid,aid)]['soc_derate']
        ready=sair[(sid,aid)]['release_slot']
        acts=actions.get(aid,[])
        for act in acts:
            if act['start']<ready: errs.append((sid,aid,'start<ready',act))
            for t in range(max(ready,0), min(act['start'],32)):
                idle_info[(aid,t)]={'loc':loc,'soc':soc}
                park[(loc,t)] += 1
            if act['type']=='charge':
                if act['origin']"'!=loc: errs.append((sid,aid,'"'charge loc mismatch',loc,act))
                fast=ar['max_charge_kw']*scenarios[sid]['charger_derate']*slot_hours
                taper=fast*config['taper_charge_fraction']
                thresh=ar['battery_capacity_kwh']*config['high_soc_threshold_fraction']
                head=max(thresh-soc,0)
                maxchg=min(fast, taper+(1-config['taper_charge_fraction'])*head, ar['battery_capacity_kwh']-soc)
                if act['energy']>maxchg+1e-6: errs.append((sid,aid,'maxchg',act['energy'],maxchg,act))
                chcnt[(loc,act['start'])]+=1; chgE[(loc,act['start'])]+=act['energy']; park[(loc,act['start'])]+=1
                soc+=act['energy']; ready=act['end']
            else:
                if act['origin']"'!=loc: errs.append((sid,aid,'"'origin mismatch',loc,act))
                rt=routes[(act['origin'],act['dest'])]
                sr=sroute[(sid,act['origin'],act['dest'])]
                if not sr['open']: errs.append((sid,aid,'route closed',act))
                exp_d=math.ceil(rt['flight_slots']*sr['flight_slot_multiplier']+scenarios[sid]['global_delay_slots']-1e-9)
                exp_e=rt['energy_kwh']*sr['energy_multiplier']
                if act['end']-act['start']<exp_d-1e-6: errs.append((sid,aid,'dur short',act,exp_d))
                if abs(act['energy']-exp_e)>1e-4: errs.append((sid,aid,'energy mismatch',act,exp_e))
                if soc+1e-6 < act['energy']+config['reserve_soc_kwh']: errs.append((sid,aid,'reserve preflight',soc,act))
                pad[(act['origin'],act['start'])]+=1
                if act['end']<32: pad[(act['dest'],act['end'])]+=1
                for t in range(act['start'],act['end']): corr[(rt['corridor_id'],t)] += 1
                noise[(act['origin'],periods[act['start']])] += rt['noise_points']*ar['noise_class']
                soc -= act['energy']
                for t in range(act['end'], min(act['end']+ar['turnaround_slots'],32)): park[(act['dest'],t)] += 1
                loc=act['dest']; ready=act['end']+ar['turnaround_slots']
        for t in range(max(ready,0),32):
            idle_info[(aid,t)]={'loc':loc,'soc':soc}
            park[(loc,t)] += 1
        for w in windows.get((sid,aid),[]):
            for t in range(w['start_slot'],w['end_slot']):
                inf=idle_info.get((aid,t))
                if not inf or inf['loc']"'!=w['"'vertiport_id']: errs.append((sid,aid,'window idle/loc',t,inf,w))
            inf=idle_info.get((aid,w['start_slot']))
            if not inf or inf['soc']+1e-6 < w['min_soc_kwh']: errs.append((sid,aid,'window soc',inf,w))
    # request windows and groups
    for rid in accepted:
        rr=req[rid]
        s=dep_times[rid]
        if not(rr['earliest_slot']<=s<=rr['latest_depart_slot']): errs.append((sid,rid,'req window'))
    for gid, rs in groups.items():
        if all(r in accepted for r in rs):
            vals=[dep_times[r] for r in rs]
            if max(vals)-min(vals) > spreads[gid]: errs.append((sid,gid,'spread',vals,spreads[gid]))
    # capacities
    for (v,t),val in pad.items():
        if val>vert[v]['pad_count']+1e-6: errs.append((sid,'pad',v,t,val,vert[v]['pad_count']))
    for (v,t),val in park.items():
        if val>vert[v]['parking_stands']+1e-6: errs.append((sid,'park',v,t,val,vert[v]['parking_stands']))
    for (v,t),val in chcnt.items():
        if val>vert[v]['charger_count']+1e-6: errs.append((sid,'charger',v,t,val,vert[v]['charger_count']))
    for (v,t),val in chgE.items():
        lim=vert[v]['grid_kw_limit']*scenarios[sid]['charger_derate']*grid[(v,t)]['grid_limit_multiplier']*slot_hours*config['charge_efficiency']
        if val>lim+1e-6: errs.append((sid,'grid',v,t,val,lim))
    for c in {r['corridor_id'] for r in routes.values()}:
        base=min(rt['corridor_capacity_per_slot'] for rt in routes.values() if rt['corridor_id']==c)
        for t in range(32):
            cap=base
            for ctl in controls.get((sid,c),[]):
                if ctl['start']<=t<ctl['end']: cap=min(cap,ctl['max'])
            if corr[(c,t)]>cap+1e-6: errs.append((sid,'corr',c,t,corr[(c,t)],cap))
    for v in vert:
        for p in ['day','evening']:
            q=vert[v]['departure_noise_quota_day'] if p=='day' else vert[v]['departure_noise_quota_evening']
            if noise[(v,p)]>q+1e-6: errs.append((sid,'noise',v,p,noise[(v,p)],q))
    for res in reserves:
        if res['scenario_id']"'!=sid: continue
        cnt=0
        for aid in aircraft:
            inf=idle_info.get((aid,res['"'slot']))
            if inf and inf['loc']==res['vertiport_id'] and inf['soc']+1e-6>=res['min_soc_kwh']:
                cnt+=1
        if cnt < res['min_idle_aircraft']:
            errs.append((sid,'reserve',res,cnt))
    print(sid,'errors',len(errs))
    for e in errs[:20]: print(' ',e)
PY"

starts={'R005':3,'R006':4,'R017':2,'R018':4,'R021':8,'R022':14,'R023':11,'R024':15,'R025':15,'R026':17,'R035':19,'R045':22,'R046':23}

with open(DATA+'/requests.csv') as f: req={r['request_id']:r for r in csv.DictReader(f)}
with open(DATA+'/routes.csv') as f: routes={(r['origin'],r['destination']):r for r in csv.DictReader(f)}
with open(DATA+'/scenarios.csv') as f: scenarios={r['scenario_id']:r for r in csv.DictReader(f)}
accept_profit=sum((int(req[r]['passengers'])*float(req[r]['fare_per_passenger']) if r in accepted else -float(req[r]['decline_penalty'])) for r in req)
for sid in scenarios:
    op=0; ch=0; delay=0
    for rid in accepted:
        rr=req[rid]; rt=routes[(rr['origin'],rr['destination'])]
        op += float(rt['operating_cost'])
        delay += float(config['delay_penalty_per_passenger_slot'])*int(rr['passengers'])*(starts[rid]-int(rr['earliest_slot']))
    for _,v,t,e in charge_plan[sid]:
        # prices equal across ports for slots used, but read exactly
        with open(DATA+'/grid_prices.csv') as f:
            pass
    prices={(r['vertiport_id'],int(r['slot'])):float(r['price_per_kwh']) for r in csv.DictReader(open(DATA+'/grid_prices.csv'))}
    ch=sum(prices[(v,t)]/float(config['charge_efficiency'])*e for _,v,t,e in charge_plan[sid])
    prof=accept_profit-op-ch-delay
    print(sid,'profit',prof)
PY"

def fmt_num(value):
    return FMT.format(float(value))


def read_csv_rows(path):
    with open(path, newline="") as f:
        return list(csv.DictReader(f))


def load_data():
    with open(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, "config.json")) as f:
        config = json.load(f)

    vertiports = {}
    for row in read_csv_rows(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, "vertiports.csv")):
        vid = row["vertiport_id"]
        vertiports[vid] = {
            "pad_count": int(row["pad_count"]),
            "charger_count": int(row["charger_count"]),
            "parking_stands": int(row["parking_stands"]),
            "grid_kw_limit": float(row["grid_kw_limit"]),
            "departure_noise_quota_day": float(row["departure_noise_quota_day"]),
            "departure_noise_quota_evening": float(row["departure_noise_quota_evening"]),
        }

    aircraft = {}
    for row in read_csv_rows(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, "aircraft.csv")):
        aid = row["aircraft_id"]
        aircraft[aid] = {
            "home_vertiport": row["home_vertiport"],
            "seat_capacity": int(row["seat_capacity"]),
            "battery_capacity_kwh": float(row["battery_capacity_kwh"]),
            "initial_soc_kwh": float(row["initial_soc_kwh"]),
            "max_charge_kw": float(row["max_charge_kw"]),
            "turnaround_slots": int(row["turnaround_slots"]),
            "noise_class": float(row["noise_class"]),
        }

    routes = {}
    for row in read_csv_rows(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, "routes.csv")):
        routes[(row["origin"], row["destination"])] = {
            "corridor_id": row["corridor_id"],
            "flight_slots": int(row["flight_slots"]),
            "energy_kwh": float(row["energy_kwh"]),
            "operating_cost": float(row["operating_cost"]),
            "noise_points": float(row["noise_points"]),
            "corridor_capacity_per_slot": int(row["corridor_capacity_per_slot"]),
        }

    requests = {}
    request_order = []
    for row in read_csv_rows(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, "requests.csv")):
        rid = row["request_id"]
        request_order.append(rid)
        requests[rid] = {
            "origin": row["origin"],
            "destination": row["destination"],
            "earliest_slot": int(row["earliest_slot"]),
            "latest_depart_slot": int(row["latest_depart_slot"]),
            "passengers": int(row["passengers"]),
            "priority_class": row["priority_class"],
            "market": row["market"],
            "fare_per_passenger": float(row["fare_per_passenger"]),
            "decline_penalty": float(row["decline_penalty"]),
        }

    scenarios = {}
    scenario_order = []
    for row in read_csv_rows(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, "scenarios.csv")):
        sid = row["scenario_id"]
        scenario_order.append(sid)
        scenarios[sid] = {
            "probability": float(row["probability"]),
            "global_delay_slots": int(row["global_delay_slots"]),
            "charger_derate": float(row["charger_derate"]),
            "soc_derate": float(row["soc_derate"]),
            "target_profit": float(row["target_profit"]),
        }

    scenario_route = {}
    for row in read_csv_rows(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, "scenario_route_availability.csv")):
        scenario_route[(row["scenario_id"], row["origin"], row["destination"])] = {
            "open": int(row["open"]),
            "flight_slot_multiplier": float(row["flight_slot_multiplier"]),
            "energy_multiplier": float(row["energy_multiplier"]),

    scenario_aircraft = {}

        scenario_aircraft[(row["scenario_id"], row["aircraft_id"])] = {

        }

    grid_prices = {}

        grid_prices[(row["vertiport_id"], int(row["slot"]))] = {
            "price_per_kwh": float(row["price_per_kwh"]),
            "grid_limit_multiplier": float(row["grid_limit_multiplier"]),
        }

    periods = {}
    for row in read_csv_rows(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, "time_slots.csv")):
        periods[int(row["slot"])] = row["period"]

    origin_floors = {}
    for row in read_csv_rows(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, "origin_service_floors.csv")):

    groups = defaultdict(list)

assistant50. Assistant message
Implemented a restoration-mode solver in [solve.py](/app/submissions/solve.py), kept the mathematical model in [model.md](/app/submissions/model.md), and produced [solution.csv](/app/submissions/solution.csv) plus [solve_log.md](/app/submissions/solve_log.md).

from itertools import product

# clear-scenario corridor scheduling for accepted C_CORE actions
jobs = {
 'REP_A07': {'dur':3,'starts':range(0,4)},
 'R005': {'dur':3,'starts':range(3,8)},
 'R006': {'dur':3,'starts':range(4,9)},
 'R007': {'dur':4,'starts':range(4,9)},
 'R009': {'dur':4,'starts':range(6,11)},
 'R011': {'dur':5,'starts':range(7,12)},
 'R021': {'dur':4,'starts':range(8,12)},
 'R022': {'dur':4,'starts':range(10,15)},
 'R023': {'dur':2,'starts':range(11,15)},
 'R024': {'dur':2,'starts':range(13,18)},
}
order = sorted(jobs, key=lambda k:(len(list(jobs[k]['starts'])), -jobs[k]['dur']))
sol={}
occ=[0]*32
best=None

def feasible_start(name,s):
    d=jobs[name]['dur']
    for t in range(s,s+d):
        if occ[t]>=2:
            return False
    # groups
    if name=='R006' and 'R005' in sol and abs(s-sol['R005'])>2:return False
    if name=='R005' and 'R006' in sol and abs(s-sol['R006'])>2:return False
    if name=='R022' and 'R021' in sol and abs(s-sol['R021'])>6:return False
    if name=='R021' and 'R022' in sol and abs(s-sol['R022'])>6:return False
    if name=='R024' and 'R023' in sol and abs(s-sol['R023'])>4:return False
    if name=='R023' and 'R024' in sol and abs(s-sol['R024'])>4:return False
    return True

def bt(i=0):
    global best
    if i==len(order):
        best=sol.copy(); print(best); raise SystemExit
    name=order[i]
    for s in jobs[name]['starts']:
        if feasible_start(name,s):
            sol[name]=s
            for t in range(s,s+jobs[name]['dur']): occ[t]+=1
            bt(i+1)
            for t in range(s,s+jobs[name]['dur']): occ[t]-=1
            del sol[name]

bt()
print('none')
PY"
